Let's detail `fast_conv`:
```
def fast_conv(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n=a.size
    if n==0: return np.array([])
    size=2*n-1
    N=1
    while N<size: N*=2
    A=np.fft.rfft(a,N)
    return np.fft.irfft(A*A,N)[:size]
```
Since a nonnegative real. Rounding small negative; max okay. Could clean `b[np.abs(b)<1e-15]=0`? not needed.

`fast_score(a)`:
```
s = a.sum(); if s <= 1e-15: return inf
a_n = a/s
m = fast_conv(a_n).max()
return 2.0 * len(a_n) * float(m)
```
For coordinates, we often keep sum1 to avoid renormalize each move. But after moves, sum persists if move zero-sum. Perturb/restrict need normalize.

`eval_safe(a)`:
```
try:
    if np.any(a < 0): a=np.maximum(a,0)

def try_move(a, s, d, fractions, current_m, best_m):
    base_sum=1
    for frac in fractions:
       amt=a[s]*frac
       if amt<=0: continue
       trial=a.copy()
       trial[s]-=amt; trial[d]+=amt; if trial[s]<0 due round set 0 renormalize?
       # sum remains 1 if no clip; but after clip? no clip unless s<0.
       m=fast_conv(trial).max()
       if m < current_m - 1e-16:
          return trial,m
    return None
```

def multiplicative_step(a, grad, tau):
   z = a * np.exp(-tau * grad / max(1,max(grad)))
   z = np.maximum(z, 0)

LP solving with scipy:
```
def solve_lp_dense(A, M, n, cap_sum, ub):
    c = -np.ones(n)
    A_ub = np.vstack((A, np.ones((1,n))))
    b_ub = np.concatenate((M*np.ones(A.shape[0]), [cap_sum]))
    bounds = [(0.0, ub) for _ in range(n)]
    res = linprog(c, A_ub=A_ub, b_ub=b_ub, bounds=bounds, method='highs', options={'presolve': True})
    if res.status == 0: return res.x
```
Potential memory: vstack copy. Could avoid by adding sum row to A initially:

```

Need ensure no nested functions. We'll define top-level `interp_weights_to_fine(weights, n)`:
```
def interp_weights_to_fine(weights, n):
    p=len(weights)
    if p==1: return np.ones(n)/n
    xw=np.linspace(0,1,p)
    xf=np.linspace(0,1,n)
    f=np.interp(xf, xw, weights)
    f=np.maximum(f,0)
    s=f.sum()
    if s<1e-15: return np.ones(n)/n
    return f/s
```

Given current b. We move ε: a_s' = a_s-ε, a_d'=a_d+ε. The change Δb = ε(2? terms) - ε² terms. Let's derive using polynomial: a' = a + ε(u_d - u_s). b' = b + ε(a*u_d + a*u_s? Actually convolution linear: b' = b + ε(conv(a, e_d) + conv(a, e_s?) times signs + ε² conv(delta, delta). Specifically conv(a + ε(e_d-e_s), same) = b + ε( conv(a,e_d)+conv(e_d,a) - conv(a,e_s)-conv(e_s,a)) + ε²( conv(e_d,e_d)-conv(e_d,e_s)-conv(e_s,e_d)+conv(e_s,e_s)). But conv(a,e_d) has coefficient a at lag i+d? conv(a,e_d)_k = a_{k-d} if valid. Since convolution symmetric, conv(a,e_d)+conv(e_d,a)=2 conv(a,e_d) except when? Δb = ε(2 shift_a_d - 2 shift_a_s) + ε²( e_d*e_d - e_d*e_s - e_s*e_d + e_s*e_s). But cross e_d*e_s appears in both? The expansion using conv: (a+δ)(a+δ)=aa+ aδ+δa+δδ. Here aδ conv = conv(a, ε(e_d-e_s)); δa = ε(e_d-e_s)*a = same, so total 2ε conv(a, e_d-e_s). δδ = ε²[(e_d)(e_d) - e_d e_s - e_s e_d + e_s e_s]. Valid indices. This O(n) update: create delta array length 2n-1 zeros, add 2ε at positions k where a_{k-d} valid, subtract 2ε where a_{k-s}. Coordinate descent with b maintenance:
- Keep `a` sum1, `b=fast_conv(a)`, `m=max(b)`.
- For each move s,d with ε, compute delta. To avoid allocation two full arrays each trial, we can update a copy of b? For line search multiple fractions, we need b_trial. We can do:
```
b_trial = b.copy()
b_trial[d:d+n] += 2*eps*a
b_trial[s:s+n] -= 2*eps*a
if 2*d < L: b_trial[2*d] += eps*eps
if 2*s < L: b_trial[2*s] += eps*eps
if d+s < L: b_trial[d+s] -= 2*eps*eps

    return None

2. `def norm_sum1(a, minimum=1e-300):` clip negative, sum.
```
def norm_sum1(a):
   a=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64).ravel()
   a=np.maximum(a,0.0)
   s=a.sum()
   if s<=1e-15: return np.ones_like(a)/a.size
   return a/s
```

3. `def simplex_project(z):` as previous. Need handle all negative, etc.

4. `def fast_conv_one(a):`
```
def fast_conv_one(a):
   a=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64)
   n=a.size
   if n==0: return np.array([],dtype=np.float64)
   size=2*n-1
   N=1<< (size-1).bit_length()
   F=np.fft.rfft(a,N)
   return np.fft.irfft(F*F,N)[:size]
```
Use bit_length: if size-1=0 -> bit_length 0? 1<<0=1 okay.

5. `def fast_conv_two(u,v):`
```
def fast_conv_two(u,v):
   n=u.size; m=v.size; size=n+m-1; N=1<<((size-1).bit_length()); return irfft(rfft(u,N)*rfft(v,N))[:size]
```

6. `def local_score(a):` returns fast_score.
```
def local_score(a):
  a=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64)
  s=a.sum()
  if s<=1e-15: return inf
  an=a/s
  m=fast_conv_one(an).max()
  return 2.0*an.size*float(m)
```

7. `def evaluate_candidate(a):`

def evaluate_candidate(a):

    a=np.maximum(a,0.0)

    return float(val)
  except Exception:
    return float('inf')
```

       if np.isfinite(val) and val < best_score:
           best_score=val; best_seq=arr.copy(); print...

13. Coordinate descent exact:
```
def coordinate_polish(a0, time_limit, verbose=False, seed=0, npeaks=30, top_peaks=8, n_dest=20, fractions=(0.01,0.02,0.05,0.1,0.2,0.4)):
    a=norm_sum1(a0); n=a.size; b=fast_conv_one(a); best_m=b.max(); best=a.copy(); cur=a.copy(); cur_m=best_m
    t0=monotonic; rng=default_rng(seed); stall=0; iter=0; last_recompute=0; eval_counter=0
    while time_left:
       iter+=1
       if iter==0 or b is None or random recompute:
          b=fast_conv_one(cur); cur_m=b.max(); if cur_m<best_m: best=cur; best_m=cur_m
       # select top peaks
       L=len(b)
       kpeaks = min(npeaks, L)
       top = np.argpartition(b, -kpeaks)[-kpeaks:]
       top = top[np.argsort(b[top])[::-1]]
       # compute weights for cost: use beta relative to spread
       bm = b[top].max(); spread = max(bm-b[top].min(), bm*1e-6)
       beta = 5.0/spread  # maybe
       w = np.exp(-(bm-b[top])*beta); w/=w.sum()
       # cost array
       cost=np.zeros(n)
       used = min(20, len(top))
       for idx in range(used):
           k=int(top[idx]); ww=float(w[idx]); i0=max(0,k-n+1); i1=min(n-1,k);
           if i0<=i1:
              iidx=np.arange(i0,i1+1); cost[iidx]+= ww* a[k-iidx]
       max_cost=cost.max(); if max_cost<=0: stall...
       # source candidates
       source_set=[]
       for pp in range(min(top_peaks, len(top))):

       for s in sources:
          if a[s]<=1e-10: continue
          for d in dest_idx:
             if d==s: continue
             # quick linear estimate: new max? skip if cost_d >= cost_s? Still try a few
             if cost[d] > cost[s] and random? skip many: if cost[d] >= cost[s]*0.9 and sources many skip? But bottom dest usually low. If not, skip.
             # Try fractions
             for frac in fractions:
                eps=a[s]*frac
                if eps<=1e-15: continue
                # update b_trial
                bt = b.copy()
                # linear additions
                # Need out of range? s+d < 2n-1 always? s+a length n; max s+n-1 <=2n-2; okay.
                bt[s:s+n] += 2*eps*a  # shift +s
                bt[d:d+n] -= 2*eps*a? Wait move from s to d: a' = a + eps(e_d - e_s). Linear = 2ε(shift_d - shift_s). So `bt[d:d+n] += 2*eps*a`; `bt[s:s+n] -= 2*eps*a`.                 if 2*s < L: bt[2*s] += eps*eps
                if 2*d < L: bt[2*d] += eps*eps
                if s+d < L: bt[s+d] -= 2*eps*eps
                mt = bt.max()

           if cur_m < best_m: best=cur.copy(); best_m=cur_m
           stall=0
       else:
           stall += 1
           # if stall > 30, try greedy projected/multiplicative
           if stall == 20 or stall%20==0:

               mcost=cost.max()

                  for tau in [0.1,0.3,1.0,3.0,10.0]:

               z = best + scale*rng.normal(size=n)
               z = np.maximum(z, 1e-12); z=z/z.sum(); mb=fast_conv_one(z).max()
               if mb < best_m * 1.03 + 1e-8/n:
                   cur=z; b=fast_conv_one(z); cur_m=mb; stall=30
               else:
                   cur=best.copy(); b=fast_conv_one(cur); cur_m=best_m; stall=0
       if iter % 200 == 0: print local score =2*n*best_m, stall, elapsed
    return best,best_m

Quadratic: δ=εe_d-εe_s. δδ = ε²(e_d e_d - e_d e_s - e_s e_d + e_s e_s). Convolution e_d e_d at 2d, e_s e_s at 2s, cross at d+s; two cross terms subtract. Good.

```
def lp_refine(a0, time_limit, verbose=False, max_lps=8, active_size=250, cap_sum=4.0, ub_factor=200):
   a=norm_sum1(a0); n=len(a); best=a; best_m=fast_conv_one(best).max(); t0...
   last_g=None
   step=0
   while time < limit and step<max_lps:
      step +=1
      b=fast_conv_one(a); M=b.max()
      active = top active_size by b (argpartition)
      if last_g is not None:
          cross=fast_conv_two(a,last_g)[:2*n-1]
          viol = np.where(cross > M + 1e-9)[0]
          if len(viol): add top viol up to active_size/2
      A = build matrix
      g = solve_lp(A, M, n, cap_sum, ub)
      if g is None: continue
      gsum=g.sum(); if gsum<=1+1e-8: maybe proceed with small? no
      g_dir=g/gsum; last_g=g_dir
      # try mixes
      improved=False
      for alpha in [0.05,0.1,0.2,0.3,0.5,0.8,1.0]:
          trial=(1-alpha)*a + alpha*g_dir
          trial=np.maximum(trial,0); ss=trial.sum(); if ss<=0 continue; trial/=ss
          mt=fast_conv_one(trial).max()
          if mt < best_m - 1e-16:
              best=trial.copy(); best_m=mt; a=trial.copy(); improved=True; break
      if not improved and verbose: print stall
   return best,best_m
```

Solve LP function:
```
def solve_lp_for_direction(A, M, n, cap_sum, ub):
    if linprog is None:
       # fallback cvxpy
    A_full = np.vstack((A, np.ones((1,n))))
    b_ub = np.append(M*np.ones(A.shape[0]), cap_sum)
    c = -np.ones(n)
    bounds = [(0.0, ub) for _ in range(n)]
    try:
       res=linprog(c, A_ub=A_full, b_ub=b_ub, bounds=bounds, method='highs', options={'presolve': True, 'dual_feasibility_tolerance':1e-8})
       if res.status==0: 
          x=np.asarray(res.x); x=np.maximum(x,0); s=x.sum(); return x if s>0 else None
    except Exception:
       pass
    # cvxpy fallback
    if cp is None: return None
    g=cp.Variable(n, nonneg=True)
    constraints=[cp.sum(g) <= cap_sum, cp.max(g) <= ub, A @ g <= M*np.ones(A.shape[0])]
    prob=cp.Problem(cp.Maximize(cp.sum(g)), constraints)
    sol=get_solver? previous _MODULE_SOLVER global? We need top-level solver selection.
       # We can test solvers quickly? Maybe too much at import. Use global _SOLVER initialized None.
```

    t0=time.monotonic(); budget=min(float(budget_s)-90.0,1740.0)

Let's design `sample_initial_candidates` concretely. Top-level. It should not be too slow.
```
def sample_initial_candidates(n, count, seed=0):
    rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
    x=np.linspace(0,1,n)
    cands=[]
    # uniform
    cands.append(np.ones(n)/n)
    for i in range(count-1):
        typ = i % 6
        if typ == 0:
            alpha=float(rng.uniform(0.2,4.0)); beta=float(rng.uniform(0.2,4.0))
            raw=(x+0.005)**alpha*(1-x+0.005)**beta
        elif typ == 1:
            p=int(rng.integers(3,16))
            alpha=float(rng.uniform(0.3,2.0))
            w=rng.dirichlet(np.full(p,alpha))
            w = w**float(rng.uniform(0.5,3.0))
            raw=interp_weights_to_fine(w,n)  # already normalized, ok
        elif typ == 2:
            m=int(rng.integers(1,4))
            raw=np.zeros(n)
            for _ in range(m):
                c=float(rng.uniform(0.05,0.95)); s=float(rng.uniform(0.03,0.25)); w=float(rng.uniform(0.3,1.0))
                raw += w*np.exp(-((x-c)**2)/(2*s*s))
        elif typ == 3:
            A=float(rng.uniform(0.1,0.9)); phi=float(rng.uniform(0.0,2*np.pi)); period=int(rng.integers(1,4))
            raw=1.0 + A*np.sin(2*np.pi*period*x/ np? domain [0,1])? Actually sin(2π*freq*x+phi)
            raw=np.maximum(raw,0.0)
        elif typ == 4:
            # random smooth cubic spline: sort random knots, heights
            p=int(rng.integers(3,10))
            knots=np.linspace(0,1,p)
            heights=rng.dirichlet(np.ones(p))*p # sum p, smooth
            raw=np.interp(x,knots,heights)
        else:
            # random positive exponential tilt of a power
            alpha=rng.uniform(0.2,3); beta=rng.uniform(0.2,3); t=rng.uniform(-3,3)
            raw=(x+0.002)**alpha*(1-x+0.002)**beta*np.exp(t*x)
        raw=np.asarray(raw,dtype=np.float64)
        if not np.all(np.isfinite(raw)): continue
        raw=np.maximum(raw,0)
        s=raw.sum()
        if s>1e-8: cands.append(raw/s)
    # remove duplicates? not necessary
    return cands
```
Need ensure no lambda. Use `np.pi`.

`def simplex_project(z):`
```
z=np.asarray(z,dtype=np.float64).copy()
n=z.size
u=np.sort(z)[::-1]
cssv=np.cumsum(u)
cond = u * np.arange(1,n+1) > (cssv - 1)
rho=np.nonzero(cond)[0]
if len(rho)==0: return np.ones(n)/n
rho_val=rho[-1]
theta=(cssv[rho_val]-1)/(rho_val+1.0)
return np.maximum(z-theta,0.0)
```
Works for simplex sum 1. If all z negative, returns uniform.

`def interp_weights_to_fine(weights,n):`
```
w=np.asarray(weights,float).ravel()
w=np.maximum(w,0); s=w.sum()
if s<=0 or n==0: return np.ones(n)/max(n,1)
w=w/s
pw=len(w)
if pw==1: return np.ones(n)
# Block exact? Use linear:
xw=np.linspace(0,1,pw)
xf=np.linspace(0,1,n)
f=np.interp(xf,xw,w)
s=f.sum(); if s<=0 uniform; return f/s
```
For p=2, linear from 0 to1, endpoints weights; okay.

old=f; old_n=len; if same copy; norm_sum1; x old/new; np.interp; normalize.
```

   if sc<best_sc: best=a; best_sc=sc
return best,best_sc
```

Now, coordinate_polish time limit: When budget slices, if time left less than 60 skip.

Let's draft coordinate_polish more precisely:

```
def coordinate_polish(a0, time_limit, verbose=False, seed=0, cost_peaks=50, source_peaks=8, n_dest=20, top_products=2, max_trials=600):
    a = norm_sum1(a0)
    n = a.size
    if n == 0: return a, inf
    b = fast_conv_one(a)
    best = a.copy(); best_m = float(b.max())
    cur = a.copy(); cur_m = best_m
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    limit = t0 + max(0.0, float(time_limit))
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    stall=0; it=0
    fractions = (0.02,0.05,0.1,0.2,0.4)
    while time.monotonic() < limit:
        it += 1
        if it == 1 or it % 50 == 0:
            b = fast_conv_one(cur)
            cm = float(b.max())
            if cm < best_m:
                best_m = cm; best = cur.copy()
            cur_m = cm
        L = len(b)
        # top peaks for cost and sources
        npk = min(cost_peaks, L)
        top = np.argpartition(b, -npk)[-npk:]
        top = top[np.argsort(b[top])[::-1]]
        bm = float(b[top[0]]) if len(top)>0 else 0.0
        # cost weights
        cost = np.zeros(n)
        if bm > 0:
            rel = b[top] / bm
            wts = rel**10.0
            wsum = wts.sum()
            if wsum <= 0: wts = np.ones_like(rel)/len(rel)
            else: wts = wts / wsum
        else: wts=...
        for tt in range(min(30, len(top))):
            k = int(top[tt]); ww = float(wts[tt])
            i0 = max(0, k - n + 1); i1 = min(n - 1, k)
            if i0 <= i1:
                ii = np.arange(i0, i1+1)
                cost[ii] += ww * a[k - ii]
        if cost.max() <= 0 and bm <= 0:
            break
        # sources
        source_list=[]
        for pp in range(min(source_peaks, len(top))):
            k=int(top[pp]); i0...; ii=...
            prod = a[ii] * a[k - ii]
            # add top product indices; choose smaller as source?
            order = np.argsort(prod)[::-1][:top_products]
            for off in order:
                i = int(ii[off]); j = k - i
                if 0 <= i < n and 0 <= j < n:
                    # add smaller of i,j? also large cost? We'll add both but sort later
                    if a[i] < 0.5*a[j]: source_list.append(i)
                    elif a[j] < 0.5*a[i]: source_list.append(j)
                    else: source_list.append(int(ii[np.argmax([a[i],a[j]])]? maybe i)
        # also top cost with mass
        mass = cur.copy() # or a
        top_cost = np.argsort(cost)[::-1][:10]
        for idx in top_cost:
            idx=int(idx)
            if cur[idx] > 1e-9: source_list.append(idx)
        # unique
        sources = np.unique(np.asarray(source_list, dtype=int))
        sources = sources[(sources>=0)&(sources<n)&(cur[sources] > 1e-9)]
        # sort by cost descending (high influence to remove)
        if len(sources)>0:
            order_s = np.argsort(cost[sources])[::-1]
            sources = sources[order_s]
        # destinations low cost + random
        dest = np.argsort(cost)[:n_dest]
        if n_dest + 10 < n:
            rand_dest = rng.integers(0,n,size=10)
            dest = np.concatenate((dest, rand_dest))
        dest = np.unique(dest)
        dest = dest[(dest>=0)&(dest<n)]
        # maybe dest exclude sources if too many? okay
        # pairs with limit
        pairs=[]
        limit_pairs = max(80, min(max_trials//len(fractions), 250))
        for s in sources[:10]:
            for d in dest[:n_dest]:
                if s==d: continue
                # simple filter: if cost[d] > cost[s] and cost[s] > 0 and cost[d] > cost[s]*0.8 skip? maybe not
                pairs.append((int(s), int(d)))
                if len(pairs) >= 250: break
            if len(pairs)>=250: break
        best_trial_m = cur_m
        best_s=-1; best_d=-1; best_frac=0.0; best_bt=None
        trials=0
        for s,d in pairs:
            if time.monotonic() > limit: break
            if cur[s] <= 1e-12: continue
            for frac in fractions:
                if time.monotonic() > limit: break
                eps = cur[s]*frac
                if eps <= 1e-15: continue
                bt = b.copy()
                bt[d:d+n] += 2.0*eps*cur
                bt[s:s+n] -= 2.0*eps*cur
                if 2*s < L: bt[2*s] += eps*eps
                if 2*d < L: bt[2*d] += eps*eps
                if s+d < L: bt[s+d] -= 2.0*eps*eps
                mt = float(bt.max())
                trials += 1
                if mt < best_trial_m - 1e-18:
                    best_trial_m=mt; best_s=s; best_d=d; best_frac=frac; best_bt=bt
                    # don't break, maybe find better
        if best_s >= 0 and best_trial_m < cur_m - 1e-16:
            cur = cur.copy()
            eps = cur[best_s]*best_frac
            cur[best_s] -= eps
            cur[best_d] += eps
            if cur[best_s] < 0: cur[best_s] = 0.0
            ssum = cur.sum()
            if ssum <= 1e-15:
                cur = best.copy(); b = fast_conv_one(cur); cur_m = best_m
            elif abs(ssum - 1.0) > 1e-12:
                cur = cur / ssum
                b = fast_conv_one(cur)
                cur_m = float(b.max())
            else:
                b = best_bt
                cur_m = best_trial_m
            if cur_m < best_m - 1e-18:
                best_m = cur_m; best = cur.copy()
            stall = 0
        else:
            stall += 1
            if stall % 25 == 0 and cost.max() > 0:
                mcost=cost.max()
                accepted=False
                for tau in (0.2,0.5,1.0,2.0,5.0,10.0):
                    z = cur * np.exp(-tau * cost / mcost)
                    z = np.maximum(z, 0.0); zs=z.sum()
                    if zs <= 0: continue
                    z = z/zs
                    mb = float(fast_conv_one(z).max())
                    if mb < best_m - 1e-16:
                        best_m=mb; best=z.copy()
                    if mb < cur_m:
                        cur=z; b=fast_conv_one(z); cur_m=mb; accepted=True; break
                if not accepted:
                    # projected
                    for eta in (0.1,0.3,1.0,3.0):
                        z = simplex_project(cur - eta*cost/mcost)
                        mb = float(fast_conv_one(z).max())
                        if mb < best_m - 1e-16:
                            best_m=mb; best=z.copy()
                        if mb < cur_m:
                            cur=z; b=fast_conv_one(z); cur_m=mb; break
            if stall > 120 or (stall > 40 and it % 40 == 0 and rng.random() < 0.2):
                scale = 0.02 + 0.01*min(3, stall//50)
                z = best + scale * rng.standard_normal(n)
                z = np.maximum(z, 0.0)
                ss = z.sum()
                if ss > 0:
                    z = z/ss
                    mb = float(fast_conv_one(z).max())
                    # accept if not too bad
                    if mb < best_m * 1.05 + 1e-9:
                        cur = z; b = fast_conv_one(z); cur_m = mb; stall = 30
                    else:
                        # try multiplicative noise
                        z2 = best * np.exp(0.03*rng.standard_normal(n))
                        z2=np.maximum(z2,0); ss=z2.sum(); if ss>0: z2/=ss
                        mb2=float(fast_conv_one(z2).max())
                        if mb2 < best_m*1.05 +1e-9:
                           cur=z2; b=fast_conv_one(z2); cur_m=mb2; stall=30
                        else:
                           cur=best.copy(); b=fast_conv_one(cur); cur_m=best_m; stall=0
        if verbose and it % 300 == 0:
            elapsed = time.monotonic()-t0
            print("    Coord it=" + str(it) + " t=" + str(round(elapsed,1)) + " J=" + repr(2*n*best_m) + " stall=" + str(stall))
    # final recompute best
    if best is not None:
        # if best sum maybe 1; recompute score for safety
        bb=fast_conv_one(best)
        best_m=float(bb.max())
    return best, best_m
```
Need check variable `a` vs `cur` in cost/source: Use `cur` not `a`. In code above source product uses `a`; should use `cur`. We'll use `cur`.

Potential problem: `bt[d:d+n] += 2.0*eps*cur` uses cur before update, correct. For multiple trials from same b, cur same. Good.

If source s >? `bt[s:s+n]` length n; okay. If d >? ok.

When source_list append: Need ensure if both i,j same (k even and i==j), product a_i^2; reducing i. `if a[i] < 0.5*a[j]` false equal; else perhaps append i. We can append i if i==j. Use:
```
if i == j:
   source_list.append(i)
else:
   if a[i] <= a[j]: source_list.append(i) # smaller or equal
   else: source_list.append(j)
   # maybe also append larger if product huge? Add both maybe with lower priority
```
We'll add both to source_list but sort by cost; too many. Add smaller only for top product to reduce. Also add top cost.

Destination bottom cost: If cost has zeros, argsort may choose random. Good. But if a destination has high a and low cost, adding mass may create peaks not in top; exact rejects. Could add low a values instead: `dest = np.argsort(cost* (1+cur*10))`? We want low cost and low existing to avoid direct high? Maybe cost captures. Use combination `dest_score = cost + 0.1*cur*max_cost`? Low cur. But if dest is zero, okay. We can compute `dest = np.argsort(cost + 0.05*max_cost*cur)[:n_dest]`.

Potential issue: In coordinate update, `bt` copy each trial; if we find a new best and continue, `best_bt` references bt; next trial creates new bt, okay. But if after loop we apply best move with best_bt, and before applying we may have changed `cur`? No.

If `best_bt` not set because no improvement, not used.

Now, LP function and `build_conv_matrix`:
```
def build_conv_matrix(a, active):
    n=a.size; K=len(active); A=np.zeros((K,n), dtype=np.float64)
    for jj in range(K):
       k=int(active[jj]); i0=max(0,k-n+1); i1=min(n-1,k)
       if i0<=i1:
          ii=np.arange(i0,i1+1)
          A[jj, ii]=a[k-ii]
    return A
```
In `lp_refine`, active top by `np.argpartition(b, -active_size)`. Unique sorted? Not needed.

`def solve_lp_direction(A, M, n, cap_sum, ub):` as above.
Need `ub` maybe if n small. If M <=0 return None.

Detailed code skeleton:

```
"""
Search for nonnegative coefficient sequence minimizing 2*n*max(conv(a,a))/(sum(a))^2.
Uses FFT for convolution, exact single-move coordinate descent with exact b updates,
 Landes, active-set LP directions, multiplicative/projected steps, random restarts,
 multi-scale/multi-length exploration.
"""
import numpy as np
import time
import math
try:
    from scipy.optimize import linprog
except Exception:
    linprog = None
try:
    import cvxpy as cp
except Exception:
    cp = None

_LP_SOLVER = None

def get_lp_solver(): ...
...
```

No lambda.

Let's design `get_lp_solver`:
```
def get_lp_solver():
    global _LP_SOLVER
    if _LP_SOLVER is not None:
        return _LP_SOLVER
    if cp is None:
        return None
    solvers = []
    try: solvers.append(cp.CLARABEL)
    except: pass
    try: solvers.append(cp.SCS)
    except: pass
    try: solvers.append(cp.ECOS)
    except: pass
    for s in solvers:
        try:
            x = cp.Variable(10, nonneg=True)
            p = cp.Problem(cp.Minimize(cp.sum(x)), [cp.sum(x) == 1, x <= 0.2])
            p.solve(solver=s, verbose=False)
            if p.status in ('optimal', 'optimal_inaccurate'):
                _LP_SOLVER = s
                return s
        except Exception:
            pass
    _LP_SOLVER = None
    return None
```

`solve_lp_direction`:
```
def solve_lp_direction(A, M, n, cap_sum, ub):
    if A is None or A.size == 0: return None
    if linprog is not None:
        try:
            A_full = np.empty((A.shape[0]+1, n), dtype=np.float64)
            A_full[:-1] = A
            A_full[-1, :] = 1.0
            b_ub = np.empty(A.shape[0]+1, dtype=np.float64)
            b_ub[:-1] = M
            b_ub[-1] = cap_sum
            c = -np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
            bounds = [(0.0, ub) for _ in range(n)]
            res = linprog(c, A_ub=A_full, b_ub=b_ub, bounds=bounds, method='highs')
            if res.status == 0 and res.x is not None:
                x = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64)
                x = np.maximum(x, 0.0)
                s = x.sum()
                if s > 1e-8:
                    return x
        except Exception:
            pass
    solver = get_lp_solver()
    if solver is None: return None
    try:
        g = cp.Variable(n, nonneg=True)
        cons = [cp.sum(g) <= cap_sum, cp.max(g) <= ub]
        if A.shape[0] > 0:
            cons.append(A @ g <= M * np.ones(A.shape[0]))
        prob = cp.Problem(cp.Maximize(cp.sum(g)), cons)
        prob.solve(solver=solver, verbose=False)
        if prob.status in ('optimal','optimal_inaccurate') and g.value is not None:
            x = np.asarray(g.value, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
            x = np.maximum(x,0)
            s=x.sum()
            if s>1e-8: return x
    except Exception:
        return None
    return None
```
Potential issue: `cp.max(g)` maybe cvxpy atom; okay. If n large, cvxpy dense A @ g expression. Fine.

`build_conv_matrix` as top-level.

`lp_refine`:
```
def lp_refine(a0, time_limit, verbose=False, max_lps=4, active_size=220, cap_sum=4.0, ub_factor=500.0):
    a=norm_sum1(a0); n=a.size
    t0=time.monotonic(); limit=t0+max(0,time_limit)
    best=a.copy(); best_m=float(fast_conv_one(best).max())
    last_dir=None; stall=0
    step=0
    while time.monotonic() < limit and step < max_lps:
        step +=1
        b=fast_conv_one(a); M=float(b.max())
        if M<=0: break
        npart=min(active_size, len(b))
        active=np.argpartition(b, -npart)[-npart:]
        active=np.unique(active)
        # active sorted maybe no
        if last_dir is not None:
            cross=fast_conv_two(a,last_dir)
            # cross length 2n-1
            thr = M + 1e-10
            viol = np.where(cross > thr)[0]
            if viol.size > 0:
                cat = np.argsort(cross[viol])[::-1][:max(20, active_size//4)]
                active = np.unique(np.concatenate((active, viol[cat])))
                if active.size > active_size + 50:
                    # keep top by b
                    order = np.argsort(b)[::-1][:active_size]
                    active = np.unique(np.concatenate((order, viol[cat])))
        A = build_conv_matrix(a, active)
        ub = ub_factor / max(n,1)
        g = solve_lp_direction(A, M, n, cap_sum, ub)
        if g is None:
            if verbose: print...
            continue
        gs=g.sum()
        if gs <= 1.0 + 1e-8:
            # maybe still use? no
            if verbose: print("LP no expansion")
            continue
        g_dir = g/gs
        last_dir = g_dir
        improved=False
        alphas = (0.02,0.05,0.1,0.2,0.35,0.5,0.8,1.0)
        for alpha in alphas:
            trial = (1.0-alpha)*a + alpha*g_dir
            trial = np.maximum(trial,0.0)
            ss=trial.sum()
            if ss <= 1e-15: continue
            trial=trial/ss
            mt=float(fast_conv_one(trial).max())
            if mt < best_m - 1e-16:
                best=trial.copy(); best_m=mt; a=trial.copy(); improved=True; break
        if not improved:
            # try from best? maybe if a not best
            if (best_m - M) > 1e-12:
                for alpha in (0.02,0.05,0.1):
                    trial=(1-alpha)*best + alpha*g_dir
                    ... if mt<best_m update
            else: stall+=1
        if verbose and step%2==0: print
    # final if a<best? return best
    return best, best_m
```
Active set update too complex; simplify: just top active each step plus previous top cross if last_dir. Use limited `max_active=active_size+100`. If active grows, trim by b.

 viol = np.where(cross > M)[0]
 if viol.size:
    viol_top = viol[np.argsort(cross[viol])[::-1][:min(50, active_size//2)]]
    active = np.unique(np.concatenate((base_active, viol_top)))
    if active.size > active_size+100:
       keep = np.argsort(b[active])[::-1][:active_size]
       active = active[keep]
```

Implement `blocks_weights_to_fine` efficiently:
```
def blocks_weights_to_fine(w,n):
    w=np.asarray(w,float).ravel(); w=np.maximum(w,0); s=w.sum(); if s<=0 return np.ones(n)/n
    w=w/s; p=len(w); base=n//p; rem=n%p; out=np.empty(n); idx=0
    for j in range(p):
        cnt=base + (1 if j<rem else 0)
        if cnt>0:
            out[idx:idx+cnt]=w[j]/cnt
            idx+=cnt
    return out
```
Sum = Σ w_j. Good.

```
def norm_sum1(a):
   a=np.asarray(a,float).ravel(); a=np.where(np.isfinite(a),a,0); a=np.maximum(a,0)
   if a.size==0: return a
   if not np.all(np.isfinite(a)): a=np.clip(a,0,1e300)
   s=a.sum()
   if not np.isfinite(s) or s<=1e-300:
      m=a.max()
      if m>0:
         a=a/m; s=a.sum()
      else: return np.ones_like(a)/a.size
   if s<=1e-300: return np.ones_like(a)/a.size
   return a/s
```

```
def coarse_softmax_optimize(n,p,maxiter,seed,style):
   if minimize is None or n<=0 or p<=0: return None
   rng=default_rng(seed)
   z0 = rng.normal(0.0,0.3,size=p)
   try:
      res = minimize(coarse_objective, z0, args=(n,p,style), method='Nelder-Mead', options={'maxiter':maxiter, 'xatol':1e-8,'fatol':1e-10,'adaptive':True})
      if res is not None:
          z = np.asarray(res.x,float).ravel()
          z = np.where(np.isfinite(z),z,0)
          m = np.max(z); w = np.exp(z-m); s=w.sum()
          if s>0: w=w/s
          else: w=np.ones(p)/p
          if style==0: a=interp_weights_to_fine(w,n)
          else: a=blocks_weights_to_fine(w,n)
          if a.size==n and np.all(np.isfinite(a)) and a.sum()>0: return a/norm? jeepers a normalized.
   except Exception: pass
   return None
```

Before final, let's scrutinize exact update formula one more time for convolution coefficient orientation. `bt[d:d+n] += 2*eps*cur`: At index d, add 2eps*cur[0]. This corresponds linear term for move to d: new coefficient at lag d = 2 * cur[0]*eps (pairs (0,d) and (d,0)). Good. At lag d+1: 2*cur[1]*eps. Good. For source subtract at s+s+i: 2*cur[i]*eps. Quadratic: at lag 2d, +eps² (pair (d,d)); at 2s, +eps²; at d+s, subtract 2eps² (pairs (d,s),(s,d)). Good.

Now, in coordinate source product for peak k, product array `prod=cur[ii]*cur[k-ii]`. For k even and i=j, product cur[i]^2, the coefficient includes a_i^2 once, but moving from i changes linear -2eps a_i, consistent. source product high. Good.

Now, if moving from source s to dest d, and s+d coefficient has both pairs, we subtract 2eps². If s+d coincides with 2s or 2d, array additions combine: e.g., s=0,d=0 not allowed; if s+d=2d? s=d not. If s+d=2s? d=s. No.

Now, use of `bt.copy()` and adding vector `2*eps*cur` to slice. The factor `2*eps*cur` length n; slice length n. Good.

Now, if d or s not int? We cast. Good.

Now, coordinate accepted move recompute sum. If sum near1, b=best_bt. But if cur after moving has tiny negative clipped to0, sum changed? We check if cur[best_s] <0 set0; if this happened, sum may be >1? Actually if cur_s - eps negative, we set to0, which increases relative to computed? The move epsilon was fraction of cur_s, can't be negative unless round/eps>cur_s. If set0, sum =1+eps-small, not exactly; our condition `abs(ssum-1)>1e-12` catches. Good.

Now, in multiplicative step, if `z.sum()` small, skip.

def sample_initial_candidates(n, count, seed=0):
    cands=[]
    if n <=0: return cands
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    x = np.linspace(0.0,1.0,n)
    cands.append(np.ones(n)/float(n))
    for i in range(max(0,count-1)):
       typ = i % 6
       raw = None
       if typ==0: ...
       elif typ==1: ...
       ...
       if raw is None: continue
       raw=np.asarray(raw,float).ravel(); 
       bad=~np.isfinite(raw); raw[bad]=0
       raw=np.maximum(raw,0); s=raw.sum()
       if s>1e-12: cands.append(raw/s)
    return cands
```

For typ4 random smooth: `knots=np.linspace(0,1,p); heights=rng.dirichlet(np.ones(p))*p`; raw=np.interp(x,knots,heights).

def coarse_optimize_candidates(n, budget, best_score_local, seed, maxiter=60):
   t0=time.monotonic(); limit=t0+budget
   best=None; bsc=inf
   styles=(0,1)
   ps=(4,6,8)
   for style in styles:
      for p in ps:
         if time.monotonic()>limit: break
         a=coarse_softmax_optimize(n,p,maxiter,seed+p*10+style,style)
         if a is None: continue
         sc=local_score(a)
         if sc<bsc: bsc=sc; best=a
         if time.monotonic()>limit: break
   return best, bsc
```

       a_out,m_out=coordinate_polish(best_seq,tlim,seed=3333,verbose=True)
       update
    # final eval choose
    if best_seq is None: fallback
    out=np.asarray(best_seq,float).ravel(); out=np.maximum(out,0.0); out=np.minimum(out,1000.0)
    s=out.sum()
    if s <= 0.01: # to avoid inf, scale
        out=np.maximum(out,0); if out.sum()==0: out=np.ones(len(out)); s=out.sum()
        out=out*10.0/s? Actually sum >1. Use out=out/s? If sum too small, scale up to 1: out=out/ s *1.0 if s>0.
    if s > 0:
        out_norm=out.copy(); out_norm=out_norm/out_norm.sum()
        val_norm=evaluate_candidate(out_norm)
        val_orig=evaluate_candidate(out)
        if np.isfinite(val_norm) and (not np.isfinite(val_orig) or val_norm < val_orig):
            out=out_norm
        # else keep out
    # ensure finite
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(out)): out fallback
    return [float(x) for x in out]
```

```python
"""
Search for a non-negative coefficient sequence minimizing
    2*n*max_k(a*a)_k / (sum(a))^2
where a*a is the linear convolution.

The implementation uses:
- FFT-based convolution for fast local evaluation.
- Exact single-coordinate moves with exact convolution updates.
- Active-set LP directions (LP Frank-Wolfe style) when SciPy/CVXPY are available.
- Soft-smoothed multiplicative and projected-gradient-like steps.
- Random restarts and multi-scale/multi-length exploration.
"""

import numpy as np
import time
import math

try:
    from scipy.optimize import linprog
except Exception:
    linprog = None

try:
    from scipy.optimize import minimize
except Exception:
    minimize = None

try:
    import cvxpy as cp
except Exception:
    cp = None

_LP_SOLVER = None


def get_lp_solver():
    global _LP_SOLVER
    if _LP_SOLVER is not None:
        return _LP_SOLVER
    if cp is None:
        return None
    solvers = []
    try:
        solvers.append(cp.CLARABEL)
    except Exception:
        pass
    try:
        solvers.append(cp.SCS)
    except Exception:
        pass
    try:
        solvers.append(cp.ECOS)
    except Exception:
        pass
    for s in solvers:
        try:
            x = cp.Variable(10, nonneg=True)
            problem = cp.Problem(cp.Minimize(cp.sum(x)), [cp.sum(x) == 1, x <= 0.2])
            problem.solve(solver=s, verbose=False)
            if problem.status in ("optimal", "optimal_inaccurate"):
                _LP_SOLVER = s
                return s
        except Exception:
            pass
    _LP_SOLVER = None
    return None


def get_parent_sequence():
    try:
        p = np.asarray(height_sequence_1, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    except Exception:
        return None
    if p.size == 0:
        return None
    p = np.where(np.isfinite(p), p, 0.0)
    p = np.maximum(p, 0.0)
    if p.sum() <= 1e-300:
        return None
    return p


def norm_sum1(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if a.size == 0:
        return a
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if not math.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
        m = float(a.max())
        if m > 0.0 and math.isfinite(m):
            a = a / m
            s = float(a.sum())
    if not math.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
        return np.ones_like(a, dtype=np.float64) / float(a.size)
    return a / s


def simplex_project(z):
    z = np.asarray(z, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = z.size
    if n == 0:
        return z
    u = np.sort(z)[::-1]
    cssv = np.cumsum(u)
    cond = u * np.arange(1, n + 1) > (cssv - 1)
    rho = np.nonzero(cond)[0]
    if rho.size == 0:
        return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / n
    rho_val = int(rho[-1])
    theta = (cssv[rho_val] - 1.0) / float(rho_val + 1)
    return np.maximum(z - theta, 0.0)


def fast_conv_one(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    size = 2 * n - 1
    N = 1 << (size - 1).bit_length()
    A = np.fft.rfft(a, N)
    return np.fft.irfft(A * A, N)[:size]


def fast_conv_two(u, v):
    u = np.asarray(u, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    v = np.asarray(v, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n, m = u.size, v.size
    if n == 0 or m == 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    size = n + m - 1
    N = 1 << (size - 1).bit_length()
    U = np.fft.rfft(u, N)
    V = np.fft.rfft(v, N)
    return np.fft.irfft(U * V, N)[:size]


def local_score(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if a.size == 0:
        return float("inf")
    s = float(a.sum())
    if not math.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
        return float("inf")
    an = a / s
    b = fast_conv_one(an)
    if b.size == 0:
        return float("inf")
    m = float(b.max())
    if not math.isfinite(m):
        return float("inf")
    return 2.0 * float(an.size) * m


def evaluate_candidate(a):
    try:
        a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        if a.size == 0:
            return float("inf")
        if not np.all(np.isfinite(a)):
            a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
        a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
        lst = [float(x) for x in a]
        val = evaluate_sequence(lst)
        return float(val)
    except Exception:
        return float("inf")


def update_best(best_seq, best_score, cand, label):
    if cand is None:
        return best_seq, best_score
    a = np.asarray(cand, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if a.size == 0:
        return best_seq, best_score
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    if a.sum() <= 1e-300:
        return best_seq, best_score

    arrs = []
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s > 1e-300:
        arrs.append(a / s)
    if np.max(a) > 1000.0:
        arrs.append(a)

    for arr in arrs:
        if arr is None or arr.size == 0:
            continue
        val = evaluate_candidate(arr)
        if math.isfinite(val) and val < best_score:
            best_score = val
            best_seq = arr.copy()
            print("  *** NEW BEST [" + str(label) + "]: " + repr(val) +
                  " (n=" + str(arr.size) + ")")
    return best_seq, best_score


def update_best_by_local_score(best_seq, best_score, best_local, cand, cand_score, label, tol=1e-9):
    if cand is None:
        return best_seq, best_score, best_local
    if best_seq is None or (math.isfinite(cand_score) and cand_score < best_local - tol):
        best_seq, best_score = update_best(best_seq, best_score, cand, label)
        if best_seq is not None:
            bl = local_score(best_seq)
            if math.isfinite(bl):
                best_local = bl
    return best_seq, best_score, best_local


def top_indices(arr, k):
    arr = np.asarray(arr)
    m = arr.size
    if k <= 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=int)
    if k >= m:
        return np.argsort(arr)[::-1]
    idx = np.argpartition(arr, -k)[-k:]
    return idx[np.argsort(arr[idx])[::-1]]


def bottom_indices(arr, k):
    arr = np.asarray(arr)
    m = arr.size
    if k <= 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=int)
    if k >= m:
        return np.argsort(arr)
    idx = np.argpartition(arr, k)[:k]
    return idx[np.argsort(arr[idx])]


def interpolate_sequence(f, new_n):
    f = norm_sum1(f)
    old_n = f.size
    if old_n == 0 or new_n <= 0:
        return np.asarray(f, dtype=np.float64).copy()
    if old_n == new_n:
        return f.copy()
    old_x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, old_n)
    new_x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, int(new_n))
    out = np.interp(new_x, old_x, f)
    return norm_sum1(out)


def interp_weights_to_fine(weights, n):
    w = np.asarray(weights, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = int(n)
    if n <= 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    w = np.where(np.isfinite(w), w, 0.0)
    w = np.maximum(w, 0.0)
    s = float(w.sum())
    if s <= 1e-300:
        return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / n
    w = w / s
    p = w.size
    if p == 0:
        return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / n
    if p == 1:
        return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / n
    xw = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, p)
    xf = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
    out = np.interp(xf, xw, w)
    out = np.maximum(out, 0.0)
    out_sum = float(out.sum())
    if out_sum <= 1e-300:
        return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / n
    return out / out_sum


def blocks_weights_to_fine(weights, n):
    w = np.asarray(weights, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = int(n)
    if n <= 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    w = np.where(np.isfinite(w), w, 0.0)
    w = np.maximum(w, 0.0)
    s = float(w.sum())
    if s <= 1e-300:
        return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / n
    w = w / s
    p = w.size
    if p == 0:
        return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / n
    if p == 1:
        return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / n
    base = n // p
    rem = n % p
    out = np.empty(n, dtype=np.float64)
    idx = 0
    for j in range(p):
        cnt = base + (1 if j < rem else 0)
        if cnt > 0:
            out[idx:idx + cnt] = w[j] / float(cnt)
            idx += cnt
    out_sum = float(out.sum())
    if out_sum <= 1e-300:
        return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / n
    return out / out_sum


def sample_initial_candidates(n, count, seed=0):
    n = int(n)
    if n <= 0:
        return []
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
    cands = [np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)]
    count = int(max(1, count))
    for i in range(count - 1):
        typ = i % 6
        raw = None
        if typ == 0:
            alpha = float(rng.uniform(0.2, 4.0))
            beta = float(rng.uniform(0.2, 4.0))
            raw = (x + 0.005) ** alpha * (1.0 - x + 0.005) ** beta
        elif typ == 1:
            p = int(rng.integers(3, 16))
            alpha = float(rng.uniform(0.3, 2.0))
            w = rng.dirichlet(np.full(p, alpha))
            sharpen = float(rng.uniform(0.5, 3.0))
            w = np.maximum(w, 0.0) ** sharpen
            raw = interp_weights_to_fine(w, n)
        elif typ == 2:
            raw = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
            mg = int(rng.integers(1, 4))
            for _ in range(mg):
                c = float(rng.uniform(0.05, 0.95))
                sig = float(rng.uniform(0.03, 0.25))
                w = float(rng.uniform(0.3, 1.0))
                raw = raw + w * np.exp(-((x - c) ** 2) / (2.0 * sig * sig))
        elif typ == 3:
            A = float(rng.uniform(0.1, 0.95))
            freq = int(rng.integers(1, 4))
            phi = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi))
            raw = 1.0 + A * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * freq * x + phi)
            raw = np.maximum(raw, 0.0)
        elif typ == 4:
            p = int(rng.integers(3, 11))
            knots = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, p)
            h = rng.dirichlet(np.ones(p)) * float(p)
            raw = np.interp(x, knots, h)
        else:
            alpha = float(rng.uniform(0.2, 3.0))
            beta = float(rng.uniform(0.2, 3.0))
            tilt = float(rng.uniform(-3.0, 3.0))
            raw = (x + 0.002) ** alpha * (1.0 - x + 0.002) ** beta * np.exp(tilt * x)
        if raw is None:
            continue
        raw = np.asarray(raw, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        raw = np.where(np.isfinite(raw), raw, 0.0)
        raw = np.maximum(raw, 0.0)
        s = float(raw.sum())
        if s > 1e-12:
            cands.append(raw / s)
    return cands


def best_candidate_from_samples(n, count, seed):
    cands = sample_initial_candidates(n, count, seed)
    best = None
    best_sc = float("inf")
    for c in cands:
        c = norm_sum1(c)
        sc = local_score(c)
        if sc < best_sc:
            best_sc = sc
            best = c
    return best, best_sc


def coarse_objective(z, n, p, style):
    z = np.asarray(z, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    z = np.where(np.isfinite(z), z, 0.0)
    if p <= 1:
        w = np.ones(max(1, int(p)), dtype=np.float64) / float(max(1, int(p)))
    else:
        m = float(z.max())
        e = np.exp(z - m)
        s = float(e.sum())
        if s <= 1e-300 or not math.isfinite(s):
            w = np.ones(int(p), dtype=np.float64) / float(p)
        else:
            w = e / s
    if int(style) == 0:
        a = interp_weights_to_fine(w, n)
    else:
        a = blocks_weights_to_fine(w, n)
    sc = local_score(a)
    if not math.isfinite(sc):
        return 1e18
    return sc


def coarse_softmax_optimize(n, p, maxiter, seed, style):
    if minimize is None or n <= 0 or p <= 0:
        return None
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    z0 = rng.normal(0.0, 0.35, int(p))
    try:
        opts = {"maxiter": int(maxiter), "xatol": 1e-8, "fatol": 1e-10, "adaptive": True}
        res = minimize(coarse_objective, z0, args=(n, p, style), method="Nelder-Mead", options=opts)
        if res is not None and hasattr(res, "x"):
            z = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
            z = np.where(np.isfinite(z), z, 0.0)
            m = float(z.max())
            e = np.exp(z - m)
            s = float(e.sum())
            if s > 1e-300:
                w = e / s
                if int(style) == 0:
                    a = interp_weights_to_fine(w, n)
                else:
                    a = blocks_weights_to_fine(w, n)
                if a.size == n and np.all(np.isfinite(a)) and a.sum() > 1e-300:
                    return a
    except Exception:
        pass
    return None


def coarse_optimize_candidates(n, time_budget, best_local, seed, maxiter=60):
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    limit = t0 + max(0.0, float(time_budget))
    best = None
    best_sc = float("inf")
    styles = (0, 1)
    ps = (4, 6, 8)
    for style in styles:
        for p in ps:
            if time.monotonic() > limit:
                break
            cand = coarse_softmax_optimize(n, p, maxiter, seed + p * 17 + style * 101, style)
            if cand is None:
                continue
            sc = local_score(cand)
            if sc < best_sc:
                best_sc = sc
                best = cand
            if time.monotonic() > limit:
                break
    return best, best_sc


def coordinate_polish(a0, time_limit, verbose=False, seed=0,
                      cost_peaks=45, source_peaks=8, n_dest=18, top_pairs=600):
    a = norm_sum1(a0)
    n = a.size
    if n <= 0 or time_limit <= 0.0:
        return a, float("inf")
    b = fast_conv_one(a)
    if b.size == 0:
        return a, float("inf")
    best = a.copy()
    best_m = float(b.max())
    cur = a.copy()
    cur_m = best_m
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    limit = t0 + float(time_limit)
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    fractions = (0.02, 0.05, 0.10, 0.20)
    stall = 0
    it = 0

    while time.monotonic() < limit:
        it += 1
        if it == 1 or it % 60 == 0:
            b = fast_conv_one(cur)
            if b.size == 0:
                break
            cm = float(b.max())
            if cm < best_m:
                best_m = cm
                best = cur.copy()
            cur_m = cm
            if not math.isfinite(cur_m) or cur_m <= 0.0:
                cur = best.copy()
                b = fast_conv_one(cur)
                cur_m = best_m

        L = b.size
        top = top_indices(b, cost_peaks)
        if top.size == 0:
            break
        bm = float(b[top[0]])
        if bm <= 0.0 or not math.isfinite(bm):
            break

        cost = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
        nfill = min(30, int(top.size))
        if nfill > 0:
            rel = np.maximum(b[top[:nfill]] / bm, 0.0)
            wts = rel ** 5.0
            wsum = float(wts.sum())
            if wsum <= 1e-300:
                wts = np.ones(nfill, dtype=np.float64) / nfill
            else:
                wts = wts / wsum
            for tt in range(nfill):
                k = int(top[tt])
                i0 = max(0, k - n + 1)
                i1 = min(n - 1, k)
                if i0 <= i1:
                    ii = np.arange(i0, i1 + 1)
                    cost[ii] += float(wts[tt]) * cur[k - ii]

        max_cost = float(cost.max())
        if max_cost <= 1e-300:
            stall += 100
        else:
            source_list = []
            nsrc = min(source_peaks, int(top.size))
            for pp in range(nsrc):
                k = int(top[pp])
                i0 = max(0, k - n + 1)
                i1 = min(n - 1, k)
                if i0 > i1:
                    continue
                ii = np.arange(i0, i1 + 1)
                prod = cur[ii] * cur[k - ii]
                if prod.size == 0:
                    continue
                order = np.argsort(prod)[::-1][:2]
                for off in order:
                    i = int(ii[off])
                    j = k - i
                    if 0 <= i < n and 0 <= j < n:
                        if i == j:
                            source_list.append(i)
                        else:
                            # reduce the smaller element of the dominant pair:
                            # first-order decrease is proportional to the product,
                            # and this keeps the perturbation smaller.
                            if cur[i] <= cur[j]:
                                source_list.append(i)
                            else:
                                source_list.append(j)

            top_cost = top_indices(cost, 8)
            for idx in top_cost:
                idx = int(idx)
                if 0 <= idx < n and cur[idx] > 1e-12:
                    source_list.append(idx)

            if source_list:
                sources = np.unique(np.asarray(source_list, dtype=int))
                mask = (sources >= 0) & (sources < n) & (cur[sources] > 1e-9)
                sources = sources[mask]
                if sources.size:
                    ord_s = np.argsort(cost[sources] * np.maximum(cur[sources], 1e-12))[::-1]
                    sources = sources[ord_s][:12]
                else:
                    sources = np.array([], dtype=int)
            else:
                sources = np.array([], dtype=int)

            dest = bottom_indices(cost, n_dest)
            if n > n_dest + 8:
                rand_dest = rng.integers(0, n, 8)
                dest = np.unique(np.concatenate((dest, rand_dest)))
            dest = np.asarray(dest, dtype=int)
            dest = dest[(dest >= 0) & (dest < n)]

            pairs = []
            max_pairs = min(int(top_pairs // len(fractions)), 220)
            max_pairs = max(max_pairs, 40)
            for s_idx in sources:
                s_idx = int(s_idx)
                if len(pairs) >= max_pairs:
                    break
                for d_idx in dest:
                    d_idx = int(d_idx)
                    if s_idx == d_idx:
                        continue
                    pairs.append((s_idx, d_idx))
                    if len(pairs) >= max_pairs:
                        break

            best_trial_m = cur_m
            best_s = -1
            best_d = -1
            best_frac = 0.0
            best_bt = None

            for s_idx, d_idx in pairs:
                if time.monotonic() > limit:
                    break
                base = cur[s_idx]
                if base <= 1e-12:
                    continue
                for frac in fractions:
                    if time.monotonic() > limit:
                        break
                    eps = base * frac
                    if eps <= 1e-15:
                        continue
                    bt = b.copy()
                    # Exact linear update for moving mass eps from s_idx to d_idx.
                    bt[d_idx:d_idx + n] += 2.0 * eps * cur
                    bt[s_idx:s_idx + n] -= 2.0 * eps * cur
                    # Quadratic terms.
                    if 2 * s_idx < L:
                        bt[2 * s_idx] += eps * eps
                    if 2 * d_idx < L:
                        bt[2 * d_idx] += eps * eps
                    if s_idx + d_idx < L:
                        bt[s_idx + d_idx] -= 2.0 * eps * eps
                    mt = float(bt.max())
                    if mt < best_trial_m - 1e-16:
                        best_trial_m = mt
                        best_s = s_idx
                        best_d = d_idx
                        best_frac = frac
                        best_bt = bt

            if best_s >= 0 and best_trial_m < cur_m - 1e-16:
                cur = cur.copy()
                eps = cur[best_s] * best_frac
                cur[best_s] -= eps
                cur[best_d] += eps
                if cur[best_s] < 0.0:
                    cur[best_s] = 0.0
                ssum = float(cur.sum())
                if ssum <= 1e-15:
                    cur = best.copy()
                    b = fast_conv_one(cur)
                    cur_m = best_m
                elif abs(ssum - 1.0) > 1e-12:
                    cur = cur / ssum
                    b = fast_conv_one(cur)
                    cur_m = float(b.max())
                else:
                    if best_bt is not None:
                        b = best_bt
                        cur_m = best_trial_m
                    else:
                        b = fast_conv_one(cur)
                        cur_m = float(b.max())
                if cur_m < best_m - 1e-18:
                    best_m = cur_m
                    best = cur.copy()
                stall = 0
            else:
                stall += 1

                if (stall == 25 or stall % 25 == 0) and max_cost > 0.0:
                    accepted = False
                    for tau in (0.2, 0.5, 1.0, 2.0, 5.0, 10.0):
                        z = cur * np.exp(-tau * cost / max_cost)
                        z = np.maximum(z, 0.0)
                        zs = float(z.sum())
                        if zs <= 1e-15:
                            continue
                        z = z / zs
                        mb = local_score(z) / (2.0 * n) if n > 0 else float("inf")
                        if mb < best_m - 1e-16:
                            best_m = mb
                            best = z.copy()
                        if mb < cur_m:
                            cur = z
                            b = fast_conv_one(cur)
                            cur_m = float(b.max())
                            accepted = True
                            break
                    if not accepted:
                        for eta in (0.1, 0.3, 1.0, 3.0):
                            z = simplex_project(cur - eta * cost / max_cost)
                            mb = local_score(z) / (2.0 * n) if n > 0 else float("inf")
                            if mb < best_m - 1e-16:
                                best_m = mb
                                best = z.copy()
                            if mb < cur_m:
                                cur = z
                                b = fast_conv_one(cur)
                                cur_m = float(b.max())
                                break

                if stall > 120 or (stall > 40 and it % 40 == 0 and rng.random() < 0.25):
                    scale = 0.02 + 0.01 * min(3, stall // 50)
                    accepted = False
                    for attempt in range(3):
                        if attempt == 0:
                            z = best + scale * rng.standard_normal(n)
                        else:
                            z = best * np.exp(0.04 * attempt * rng.standard_normal(n))
                        z = np.maximum(z, 0.0)
                        zs = float(z.sum())
                        if zs <= 1e-15:
                            continue
                        z = z / zs
                        mb = local_score(z) / (2.0 * n) if n > 0 else float("inf")
                        tol_m = best_m * (1.02 + 0.01 * min(3, stall // 100))
                        if mb < tol_m and math.isfinite(mb):
                            cur = z
                            b = fast_conv_one(cur)
                            cur_m = float(b.max())
                            stall = 30
                            accepted = True
                            break
                    if not accepted:
                        cur = best.copy()
                        b = fast_conv_one(cur)
                        cur_m = best_m
                        stall = 0

        if verbose and it % 300 == 0:
            el = time.monotonic() - t0
            print("    Coord it=" + str(it) + " t=" + str(round(el, 1)) +
                  " J=" + repr(2.0 * n * best_m) + " stall=" + str(stall))

    if best.size > 0:
        bb = fast_conv_one(best)
        if bb.size > 0:
            best_m = float(bb.max())
    return best, best_m


def build_conv_matrix(a, active):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    active = np.asarray(active, dtype=int).ravel()
    n = a.size
    K = active.size
    A = np.zeros((K, n), dtype=np.float64)
    for jj in range(K):
        k = int(active[jj])
        i0 = max(0, k - n + 1)
        i1 = min(n - 1, k)
        if i0 <= i1:
            ii = np.arange(i0, i1 + 1)
            A[jj, ii] = a[k - ii]
    return A


def solve_lp_direction(A, M, n, cap_sum, ub):
    if A is None or A.size == 0 or n <= 0:
        return None
    if M <= 1e-300:
        return None

    if linprog is not None:
        try:
            A_full = np.empty((A.shape[0] + 1, n), dtype=np.float64)
            A_full[:-1] = A
            A_full[-1, :] = 1.0
            b_ub = np.empty(A.shape[0] + 1, dtype=np.float64)
            b_ub[:-1] = M
            b_ub[-1] = cap_sum
            c = -np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
            bounds = [(0.0, ub) for _ in range(n)]
            res = linprog(c, A_ub=A_full, b_ub=b_ub, bounds=bounds, method="highs")
            if res is not None and getattr(res, "status", 1) == 0 and res.x is not None:
                x = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
                x = np.maximum(x, 0.0)
                s = float(x.sum())
                if s > 1e-8:
                    return x
        except Exception:
            pass

    solver = get_lp_solver()
    if solver is None:
        return None
    try:
        g = cp.Variable(n, nonneg=True)
        cons = [cp.sum(g) <= cap_sum, cp.max(g) <= ub]
        if A.shape[0] > 0:
            cons.append(A @ g <= M * np.ones(A.shape[0]))
        problem = cp.Problem(cp.Maximize(cp.sum(g)), cons)
        problem.solve(solver=solver, verbose=False)
        if problem.status in ("optimal", "optimal_inaccurate") and g.value is not None:
            x = np.asarray(g.value, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
            x = np.maximum(x, 0.0)
            s = float(x.sum())
            if s > 1e-8:
                return x
    except Exception:
        pass
    return None


def lp_refine(a0, time_limit, verbose=False, max_lps=3, active_size=200,
              cap_sum=4.0, ub_factor=500.0):
    a = norm_sum1(a0)
    n = a.size
    if n <= 0 or time_limit <= 0.0 or n > 10000:
        return a, float("inf")
    b = fast_conv_one(a)
    best = a.copy()
    best_m = float(b.max())
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    limit = t0 + float(time_limit)
    last_dir = None

    for step in range(int(max_lps)):
        if time.monotonic() > limit:
            break
        b = fast_conv_one(a)
        M = float(b.max())
        if M <= 1e-15 or not math.isfinite(M):
            break

        base_active = top_indices(b, active_size)
        active = base_active
        if last_dir is not None:
            cross = fast_conv_two(a, last_dir)
            cross = cross[:2 * n - 1]
            thr = M + 1e-10
            viol = np.where(cross > thr)[0]
            if viol.size > 0:
                order_v = np.argsort(cross[viol])[::-1][:min(50, max(20, active_size // 4))]
                vt = viol[order_v]
                active = np.unique(np.concatenate((base_active, vt)))
                if active.size > active_size + 100:
                    active = np.unique(np.concatenate((base_active, vt[:20])))
                    if active.size > active_size + 50:
                        active = base_active

        if active.size == 0:
            continue

        A = build_conv_matrix(a, active)
        ub = max(100.0 / n, 0.01)
        g = solve_lp_direction(A, M, n, cap_sum, ub)
        if g is None:
            continue
        gs = float(g.sum())
        if gs <= 1.0 + 1e-8:
            if verbose:
                print("    LP no expansion (sum=" + str(gs) + ")")
            continue
        g_dir = g / gs
        last_dir = g_dir

        improved = False
        alphas = (0.02, 0.05, 0.10, 0.20, 0.35, 0.50, 0.80, 1.00)
        for alpha in alphas:
            if time.monotonic() > limit:
                break
            trial = (1.0 - alpha) * a + alpha * g_dir
            trial = np.maximum(trial, 0.0)
            ts = float(trial.sum())
            if ts <= 1e-15:
                continue
            trial = trial / ts
            mt = local_score(trial) / (2.0 * n) if n > 0 else float("inf")
            if mt < best_m - 1e-16:
                best = trial.copy()
                best_m = mt
                a = trial.copy()
                improved = True
                break

        if not improved and verbose:
            print("    LP step=" + str(step) + " no immediate J improvement; J=" + repr(2.0 * n * best_m))

    if best.size > 0:
        bb = fast_conv_one(best)
        if bb.size > 0:
            best_m = float(bb.max())
    return best, best_m


def propose_candidate(budget_s=1800.0):
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    try:
        budget = min(float(budget_s) - 120.0, 1740.0)
    except Exception:
        budget = 1740.0
    if budget < 60.0:
        budget = max(30.0, float(budget_s) - 10.0)

    best_seq = None
    best_score = float("inf")
    best_local = float("inf")

    p = get_parent_sequence()
    if p is not None:
        best_seq, best_score = update_best(best_seq, best_score, p, "parent")

    if best_seq is None:
        n_init = 7000
        x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n_init)
        raw = (x + 0.01) ** 1.2 * (1.0 - x + 0.01) ** 0.8
        best_seq = norm_sum1(raw)
        best_score = evaluate_candidate(best_seq)
        if not math.isfinite(best_score):
            best_seq = norm_sum1(np.ones(n_init, dtype=np.float64))
            best_score = evaluate_candidate(best_seq)

    if best_seq is None:
        best_seq = norm_sum1(np.ones(7000, dtype=np.float64))
        best_score = evaluate_candidate(best_seq)

    best_local = local_score(best_seq)
    if not math.isfinite(best_local):
        best_local = float("inf")

    print("Initial: n=" + str(len(best_seq)) + ", eval=" + repr(best_score) +
          ", local=" + repr(best_local))

    # Initial diverse sampling.
    remaining = budget - (time.monotonic() - t0)
    if remaining > 30:
        cand, cand_sc = best_candidate_from_samples(len(best_seq), 25, seed=101)
        if cand is not None:
            best_seq, best_score, best_local = update_best_by_local_score(
                best_seq, best_score, best_local, cand, cand_sc, "sample_init")

    # Coarse optimization to find a better basin.
    remaining = budget - (time.monotonic() - t0)
    if remaining > 120:
        cand, cand_sc = coarse_optimize_candidates(
            len(best_seq), min(remaining * 0.045, 90.0), best_local, 211, maxiter=70)
        if cand is not None:
            best_seq, best_score, best_local = update_best_by_local_score(
                best_seq, best_score, best_local, cand, cand_sc, "coarse_1")

    # Main exact local polish.
    remaining = budget - (time.monotonic() - t0)
    if remaining > 180:
        tlim = min(remaining * 0.22, 360.0)
        out, mout = coordinate_polish(best_seq, tlim, verbose=True, seed=7)
        sc = 2.0 * len(out) * mout if mout is not None and math.isfinite(mout) else float("inf")
        best_seq, best_score, best_local = update_best_by_local_score(
            best_seq, best_score, best_local, out, sc, "coord_1")

    # Explore a few different basins briefly.
    for seed in (217, 331, 443):
        remaining = budget - (time.monotonic() - t0)
        if remaining < 120:
            break
        cand, cand_sc = best_candidate_from_samples(len(best_seq), 14, seed)
        if cand is None:
            continue
        if math.isfinite(best_local) and cand_sc > best_local * 1.05:
            continue
        tlim = min(remaining * 0.05, 70.0)
        out, mout = coordinate_polish(cand, tlim, verbose=False, seed=seed)
        sc = 2.0 * len(out) * mout if mout is not None and math.isfinite(mout) else float("inf")
        best_seq, best_score, best_local = update_best_by_local_score(
            best_seq, best_score, best_local, out, sc, "diverse_" + str(seed))

    # LP direction refinement.
    remaining = budget - (time.monotonic() - t0)
    if remaining > 160 and len(best_seq) <= 9000:
        tlim = min(remaining * 0.07, 120.0)
        out, mout = lp_refine(best_seq, tlim, verbose=True, max_lps=2, active_size=200)
        sc = 2.0 * len(out) * mout if mout is not None and math.isfinite(mout) else float("inf")
        best_seq, best_score, best_local = update_best_by_local_score(
            best_seq, best_score, best_local, out, sc, "lp_1")

    # Another coarse pass, then exact polish if useful.
    remaining = budget - (time.monotonic() - t0)
    if remaining > 180:
        cand, cand_sc = coarse_optimize_candidates(
            len(best_seq), min(remaining * 0.05, 100.0), best_local, 501, maxiter=60)
        if cand is not None:
            best_seq, best_score, best_local = update_best_by_local_score(
                best_seq, best_score, best_local, cand, cand_sc, "coarse_2")

    remaining = budget - (time.monotonic() - t0)
    if remaining > 220:
        tlim = min(remaining * 0.30, 430.0)
        out, mout = coordinate_polish(best_seq, tlim, verbose=True, seed=801)
        sc = 2.0 * len(out) * mout if mout is not None and math.isfinite(mout) else float("inf")
        best_seq, best_score, best_local = update_best_by_local_score(
            best_seq, best_score, best_local, out, sc, "coord_2")

    # Multi-length exploration.
    base_n = len(best_seq)
    tried = set()
    for dn in (500, 1000, -400, 1500, -800, 2000):
        remaining = budget - (time.monotonic() - t0)
        if remaining < 180:
            break
        nl = base_n + dn
        if nl in tried:
            continue
        if not (4000 <= nl <= 9000):
            continue
        tried.add(nl)
        a_new = interpolate_sequence(best_seq, nl)
        cand, cand_sc = best_candidate_from_samples(nl, 10, 1000 + int(nl))
        if cand is not None:
            lc = local_score(cand)
            ln = local_score(a_new)
            if cand_sc < ln:
                a_new = cand
        tlim = min(remaining * 0.06, 75.0)
        out, mout = coordinate_polish(a_new, tlim, verbose=False, seed=2000 + int(nl))
        sc = 2.0 * len(out) * mout if mout is not None and math.isfinite(mout) else float("inf")
        best_seq, best_score, best_local = update_best_by_local_score(
            best_seq, best_score, best_local, out, sc, "len_" + str(nl))

    # Final small LP.
    remaining = budget - (time.monotonic() - t0)
    if remaining > 100 and len(best_seq) <= 9000:
        tlim = min(remaining * 0.15, 90.0)
        out, mout = lp_refine(best_seq, tlim, verbose=True, max_lps=1, active_size=150)
        sc = 2.0 * len(out) * mout if mout is not None and math.isfinite(mout) else float("inf")
        best_seq, best_score, best_local = update_best_by_local_score(
            best_seq, best_score, best_local, out, sc, "lp_final")

    # Final exact polish.
    remaining = budget - (time.monotonic() - t0)
    if remaining > 60:
        tlim = min(remaining * 0.9, 200.0)
        out, mout = coordinate_polish(best_seq, tlim, verbose=True, seed=9091)
        sc = 2.0 * len(out) * mout if mout is not None and math.isfinite(mout) else float("inf")
        best_seq, best_score, best_local = update_best_by_local_score(
            best_seq, best_score, best_local, out, sc, "final_coord")

    if best_seq is None:
        best_seq = norm_sum1(np.ones(7000, dtype=np.float64))

    out = np.asarray(best_seq, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    out = np.where(np.isfinite(out), out, 0.0)
    out = np.maximum(out, 0.0)
    out = np.minimum(out, 1000.0)
    s = float(out.sum())
    if s <= 1e-12:
        out = np.ones(max(1, len(out)) if len(out) > 0 else 7000, dtype=np.float64)
        s = float(out.sum())
    if 0.0 < s < 0.01:
        out = out / s
        s = 1.0

    if s > 0.0:
        out_norm = out / float(out.sum())
        val_norm = evaluate_candidate(out_norm)
        val_orig = evaluate_candidate(out)
        if math.isfinite(val_norm) and (not math.isfinite(val_orig) or val_norm < val_orig):
            out = out_norm
        else:
            out = out
    else:
        out = norm_sum1(np.ones(7000, dtype=np.float64))

    if not np.all(np.isfinite(out)):
        out = norm_sum1(np.ones(7000, dtype=np.float64))

    final_val = evaluate_candidate(out)
    print("FINAL: n=" + str(len(out)) + ", eval=" + repr(final_val) +
          ", runtime=" + str(round(time.monotonic() - t0, 1)) + "s")

    return [float(x) for x in out]
```
